CREATE TABLE departments (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	short_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	expert1_id INTEGER, 
	expert2_id INTEGER, 
	director_faculty_id INTEGER, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	UNIQUE (short_code)
)

CREATE TABLE users (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	email VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	hashed_password VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	designation VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	gender VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, 
	role_id INTEGER, 
	department_id INTEGER, 
	is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	signature_path VARCHAR(500), 
	is_approved BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	last_login_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE, 
	created_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id), 
	UNIQUE (email), 
	FOREIGN KEY(role_id) REFERENCES role_managers (id), 
	FOREIGN KEY(department_id) REFERENCES departments (id)
)


INFO     sqlalchemy.engine.Engine:base.py:1846 [no key 0.00004s] ()
INFO     sqlalchemy.engine.Engine:base.py:1846 
CREATE TABLE purchase_categories (
	id SERIAL NOT NULL, 
	title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, 
	min_amount FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	max_amount FLOAT NOT NULL, 
	is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL, 
	procurement_id INTEGER NOT NULL, 
	requirement_type VARCHAR(100), 
	created_at TIMESTAMP WITHOUT TIME ZONE NOT NULL, 
	PRIMARY KEY (id),
